                             "ariaLabel": "",
"helpText": "## Prescription Record AuthorizationlnlnIf you have any questions or need any assistance, please contact us.InlnBy clicking I"I agreel" below, youacknowledge that you can access, read, and print the following terms and conditions.InlnPrescription Drug Record Authorization Prescription Drug Record Authorization Iauthorize any pharmacy benefit manager or other pharmaceutical firm having information about myprescription drug records to give all information to MEMBERs Life Insurance Company(\"Company\") to determine eligibility for insurance or benefits. Information obtained will bereleased only to persons performing business duties as delegated or contracted for by theCompany related to my application and subsequent insurance-related functions, as permitted orrequired by law, or as I further authorize. The health information shared for these purposes isnot subject to federal health information privacy laws; however state privacy laws do apply. Iagree this authorization is valid for 24 months or such time limit as provided by applicablestate law, a copy is as valid as the original, and I or my authorized representative canreceive a copy upon request. For purposes of collecting information in connection with a claimfor benefits, this Authorization is valid for the duration of the claim. I understand that: (1)I can revoke this authorization at any time by written request to the Company; (2) revocationof this authorization will not affect any prior action taken by the Company in reliance uponthis authorization; and (3) failure to sign, or revocation of this authorization may impair theCompany's ability to evaluate claims or process applications and may be a basis for denyingthis application or a claim for benefits. The Notice to Applicant has been received by me.",

                             "arialabel": "",
"helpText": "## Replacing Your Life Insurance Policy?InlnAre you thinking about buying a new policy and discontinuing or changing an existing policy? If youare, your decision could be a good one - or a mistake. You will not know for sure unless youmake a careful comparison of your existing policy and the proposed policy.InlnMake sure youunderstand the facts. Below you will find a check list of some of the items you should considerin making your decision. TAKE TIME To READ IT.InlnDo not let one agent or insurer prevent youfrom obtaining information from another agent or insurer which may be to your advantage.InHearboth sides before you decide. This way you can be sure you are making a decision that is inyour best interest.Inwe are required to notify your existing company that you may be replacingtheir policy.InlnITEMs To CONSIDERInIf the policy coverages are basically similar, premiums fora new policy may be higher because rates increase as your age increases.InCash values anddividends, if any, may grow slower under a new policy initially because of the initial costs ofissuing a policy.InYour present insurance company may be able to make a change on terms whichmay be more favorable than if you replace existing insurance with new insurance.InIf you borrowagainst an existing policy to pay premiums on a new policy, death benefits payable under yourexisting policy will be reduced by the amount of any unpaid loan, including unpaid interest.Incurrent interest rates are not guaranteed. Guaranteed interest rates are usuallyconsiderably lower than current rates. what rates are guaranteed?lnAre premiums guaranteed orsubject to change - up or down?nParticipating policies pay dividends that may materiallyreduce the cost of insurance over the life of the contract. Dividends, however, are notguaranteed.InlnCAuTIoN, you are urged not to take action to terminate, assign, or alter yourexisting life insurance coverage until after you have been issued the new policy, examined itand have found it to be acceptable to you.InlnAnd, REMEMBER, you have thirty (3o) daysfollowing receipt of any life insurance policy to examine its contents. # Mock Public Data

At the moment, this is used for simulating Chase HPF for storybook.

## Regenerating the iframe HTML

If you're in the Chase portal on the page to edit a form, you can run this Js to grab thestring contents of the iframe.

js
document.getElementById('previewIframe').attributes.srcdoc.value

```typescript
/* eslint-disable class-methods-use-this */
import TagManager from 'react-gtm-module';
import appConfig from 'api/appConfig';
import { analyticsInputChangeUtils } from './inputChangeUtils';
import {
AnalyticsEvent,
IAnalyticsApplication,
IAnalyticsAdTrax,
IAnalyticsAppAddressScrubbing,
IAnalyticsFieldError,
IAnalyticsBanner,
IAnalyticsRiderDisplay,
IAnalyticsProgressItemListClick,
IAnalyticsPixelFire,
} from './types';

export class AnalyticsService {
private static instance: AnalyticsService;

    constructor() {
this.bootstrapGoogleTagManager();

        document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.ADDRESS_SCRUBBING, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.applicationAddressScrubbing(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.ADTRAX, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.adTraxOnPage(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.APPLICATION_FUNNEL, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.application(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.CHECKBOX_INTERACTION, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.applicationInputChange(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.HEALTH_QUESTIONS, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.applicationInputChange(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.HELP_TEXT, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.applicationInputChange(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.INPUT, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.applicationInputChange(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.OTHER_BUTTONS, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.applicationInputChange(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.RIDER_DISPLAYED, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.riderDisplay(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.RIDER_DISPLAYED_SELECTED, ((e: CustomEvent) =>
this.riderDisplay(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.RIDER_REMOVED, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.riderDisplay(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.RIDER_SELECTED, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.riderDisplay(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.SELECTION, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.applicationInputChange(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.FIELD_ERROR, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.fieldError(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.ERROR_BANNER, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.notificationBanner(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.ALERT_BANNER, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.notificationBanner(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.USER_DATA, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.userData(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.ERROR_LOADING_DOC, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.application(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.ABANDON_APP, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.application(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.PROGRESS_ITEM_LIST_BUTTON, ((e: CustomEvent)
this.progressItemListButtonClick(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.PIXEL_FIRE, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.startPixelFire(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.SAFETY_NET_TIMER_DISPLAYED, ((e: CustomEvent)
this.basicEvent(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.SAFETY_NET_TIMER_UPDATED, ((e: CustomEvent) =>
this.basicEvent(e);
}) as EventListener);
document.addEventListener(AnalyticsEvent.CARRIER_NAMES_LOOKUP_SYSTEM_DOwN, ((e: CustomEvent) => {
this.basicEvent(e);
}) as EventListener);
}

    [>>
* Use this to establish Analytics Service once per project.
     >[
public static init(): AnalyticsService {
if (!AnalyticsService.instance) {
AnalyticsService.instance = new AnalyticsService();
}
return AnalyticsService.instance;
}

    private bootstrapGoogleTagManager = (): void => {
const tagManagerArgs = {
gtmId: appConfig.REACT_APP_GOOGLE_TAG_MANAGER_ID || '',
};
if (tagManagerArgs.gtmId !== '') {
TagManager.initialize(tagManagerArgs);
}
};

    private application = (
event:
| GlobalEventHandlersEventMap[AnalyticsEvent.APPLICATION_FUNNEL]
| GlobalEventHandlersEventMap[AnalyticsEvent.ERROR_LOADING_DOC]
| GlobalEventHandlersEventMap[AnalyticsEvent.ABANDON_APP]
) => {
const { detail } = event;
const  channel, coverageAmount, policyStatus, premiumAmount, productCode, segmentTitle
detail as IAnalyticsApplication;
TagManager.dataLayer({
dataLayer: {
event: event.type,
stepName: segmentTitle.replace('&', 'and'), // sending symbols in the data might mess up things in GTM when using Regex comparisions
channel,
...(policyStatus !== undefined && { policyStatus }),
productCode,
premiumAmount,
coverageAmount,
},
        }];
};

    private basicEvent = (
event:
| GlobalEventHandlersEventMap[AnalyticsEvent.SAFETY_NET_TIMER_DISPLAYED]
| GlobalEventHandlersEventMap[AnalyticsEvent.SAFETY_NET_TIMER_UPDATED]
| GlobalEventHandlersEventMap[AnalyticsEvent.CARRIER_NAMES_LOOKUP_SYSTEM_DOWN]
) => {
TagManager.dataLayer({
dataLayer: {
event: event.type,
},
        }];
};

    private adTraxOnPage = (event: GlobalEventHandlersEventMap[AnalyticsEvent.ADTRAX]) => {
const { detail } = event;
const { adTraxValue } = detail as IAnalyticsAdTrax;
TagManager.dataLayer({
dataLayer: {
```

```typescript
/* eslint-disable class-methods-use-this */
import { LoanApplicationEntities as InsuranceApplicationEntities } from 'commonui';
import { AnalyticsDataFieldoptionsType, IInsuranceApp, FieldCode, FieldGroupCode  from'types/customTypes';
import { formatOrdinals } from 'utils/formatters';
import { fireApplicationInputChangeEvent, fireAppStepperButtonclickEvent  from'./fireEventutils';
import { IAnalyticsDataField, IAnalyticsAppInputChange } from './types';
import { AnalyticsEvent } from './index';

class AnalyticsButtonClickUtils {
public runAnalytics = (
dataFieldMap: Map<string, InsuranceApplicationEntities.IDataField>,
insuranceApp: IInsuranceApp | undefined,
fieldCode: string,
value?: string
) => {
let eventData;
let delay = false;
switch (value) {
case 'addGroup':
eventData = this.getAddGroupEventData(fieldCode, dataFieldMap);
delay = true;
break;
case 'helpText':
eventData = this.getHelpTextEventData(fieldCode, dataFieldMap, 'Clicked');
break;
case 'helpTextClosed':
eventData = this.getHelpTextEventData(fieldCode, dataFieldMap, 'Closed');
break;
case 'removeGroup':
eventData = this.getRemoveGroupEventData(fieldCode, dataFieldMap);
delay = true;
break;
default: {
const analyticsDataField = dataFieldMap.get(fieldCode);
if (analyticsDataField) {
eventData = this.getEventData(analyticsDataField as IAnalyticsDataField);
}
}
}
if (eventData) {
fireApplicationInputChangeEvent(
eventData.eventName,
eventData.fieldName,
eventData.fieldAction,
insuranceApp,
delay,
false
            };
}
};

    public runStepperClick = (
insuranceApp: IInsuranceApp,
isIncrement: boolean,
updatedCoverage: number,
updatedPremium: number,
rider?: {
name: string;
productCode: string;
updatedPremium: number;
updatedCoverage: number;
}
) => {
if (!insuranceApp || !updatedCoverage || !updatedPremium) return;

        const evtName = AnalyticsEvent.OTHER_BUTTONS;
const { segment, googleAnalyticsData } = insuranceApp;
const { segmentTitle } = segment;

        let fieldName = `${isIncrement ? 'Increase' : 'Decrease'} Rate`;
if (rider) {
fieldName = `${rider.name} Rider ${isIncrement ? 'Increase' : 'Decrease'} Rate`;
}

        const detail: IAnalyticsAppInputChange = {
productCode: googleAnalyticsData.productCode || '',
channel: googleAnalyticsData.channel || 'Web',
premiumAmount: updatedPremium.toFixed(2),
coverageAmount: updatedCoverage.toString(),
segmentTitle: segmentTitle || '',
fieldName,
fieldAction: 'Clicked',
...(rider && {
riderProductCode: rider.productCode,
riderPremiumAmount: rider.updatedPremium.toFixed(2),
riderCoverageAmount: rider.updatedCoverage.toString(),
            }],
};

        fireAppStepperButtonClickEvent(evtName, detail);
};

    private getEventData = (
analyticsDataField: IAnalyticsDataField
): { eventName: AnalyticsEvent; fieldName: string; fieldAction: string } | undefined => {
if (!analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics) return undefined;
const eventName = analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics.eventName as AnalyticsEvent;
let fieldAction = '';
let fieldName = '';
if (analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics.optionsType === AnalyticsDataFieldOptionsType.CLICK){
fieldAction = 'Clicked';
fieldName = analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics.fieldName;
}
        if (eventName && fieldName && fieldAction) {
            return { eventName, fieldName, fieldAction };
        }
        return undefined;
    };

    private getHelpTextEventData = (
        fieldCode: string.
        dataFieldMap: Map<string, InsuranceApplicationEntities.IDataField>,
        action: string = 'clicked'
    ): { eventName: AnalyticsEvent; fieldName: string: fieldAction: string  | undefined => {
        const eventName = AnalyticsEvent.HELP_TEXT;
        const fieldAction = action;
        let fieldName = -';
        const dataField = dataFieldMap.get(fieldCode);
        if ((dataField){
            fieldName = dataField.fieldLabel;
        }
        if (fieldCode.startsWith(FieldGroupCode.ADD_BENEFICIARIES)) {
            fieldName = 'Add Beneficiaries';
        }
        if (fieldCode === FieldCode.SECONDARY_ADDRESSEE){
            fieldName = 'Secondary Addressee';
        }
        if (eventName && fieldName && fieldAction) {
            return  eventName, fieldName, fieldAction };
        }
        return undefined;
    };

    private getAddGroupEventData = (
        fieldCode: string,
        dataFieldMap: Map<string, InsuranceApplicationEntities.IDataField>
    ): { eventName: AnalyticsEvent; fieldName: string; fieldAction: string } | undefined => {
        const eventName = AnalyticsEvent.OTHER_BUTTONS;
        let fieldAction = -';
        let fieldName = '';
        const dataField = dataFieldMap.get(fieldCode);
        if(dataField){
            fieldName = dataField.fieldLabel;
        }
        if (fieldCode.startsWith(FieldCode.ADD_OTHER_BENEFICIARY)) {
            const groupNumber = parseInt(fieldCode[fieldCode.length - 1], 1o);
            fieldName = 'Add Beneficiary';
            fieldAction = '${formatordinals(groupNumber + 2)} beneficiary';
        }
        if (fieldCode.startsWith(FieldCode.ADD_OTHER_EXISTING_COVERAGE)) {
            const groupNumber = parseInt(fieldCode[fieldCode.length - 1, 1o);
            fieldName = 'Add Another Policy';
            fieldAction = '${formatordinals(groupNumber + 2)} policy';
        }
        if (eventName && fieldName && fieldAction) {
            return { eventName, fieldName, fieldAction };
        }
        return undefined;
    };

    private getRemoveGroupEventData = (
        fieldCode: string.
        dataFieldMap: Map<string, InsuranceApplicationEntities.IDataField>
    ): { eventName: AnalyticsEvent; fieldName: string: fieldAction: string  I undefined => {
        const eventName = AnalyticsEvent.OTHER_BUTTONS;
        let fieldAction = -';
        let fieldName = -';
        const dataField = dataFieldMap.get(fieldCode):
        if (dataField) {
            fieldName = dataField.fieldLabel;
        }
        if (fieldCode.startsWith(FieldGroupCode.ADD_BENEFICIARIES_HIDDEN)) {
            const groupNumber = parseInt(fieldCode[fieldCode.length - 1, 1o);
            fieldName = 'Remove Beneficiary';
            fieldAction = '${formatordinals(groupNumber + 1)} beneficiary';
        }
        if (fieldCode.startsWith(FieldGroupCode.ADD_EXISTING_COVERAGE_HIDDEN)) {
            const groupNumber = parseInt(fieldCode[fieldCode.length - 1, 1o);
            fieldName = 'Remove Policy';
            fieldAction = '${formatordinals(groupNumber + 1)} policy';
        }
           (eventName && fieldName && fieldAction) {
            return { eventName, fieldName, fieldAction };
        }
        return undefined;
    };
}

export const analyticsButtonclickutils = new AnalyticsButtonclickutils();
```

import { IInsuranceApp, IInsuranceAppSummary, IExternalAppSummary } from 'types/customTypes';
import { IAnalyticsDataField } from 'services/Analytics';
import { getAppFinishEventStepName, getAppFinishEventPolicyStatus, setGlobalGAVariables } from'./utils';
import { analyticsInputChangeutils } from './inputChangeutils';
import {
    AnalyticsEvent,
    IAnalyticsAdTrax,
    IAnalyticsApplication,
    IAnalyticsAppInputChange,
    IAnalyticsAppAddressScrubbing,
    IAnalyticsFieldError,
    IAnalyticsBanner,
    IAnalyticsUserData,
    IAnalyticsRiderDisplay,
    IAnalyticsProgressItemListClick,
    IAnalyticsPixelFire,
} from './types';

export const fireApplicationEvent = (evtName: AnalyticsEvent, insuranceApp?: IInsuranceApp) => {
    if (!insuranceApp) return;

    const { segment, googleAnalyticsData } = insuranceApp;
    const { segmentTitle } = segment;

    const detail: IAnalyticsApplication = {
        productCode: googleAnalyticsData.productCode ll -',
        channel: googleAnalyticsData.channel ll 'Web',
        premiumAmount: googleAnalyticsData.premiumAmount Il -',
        coverageAmount: googleAnalyticsData.coverageAmount ll ',
        segmentTitle: segmentTitle ll -',
    };

    // set global variable for GTM to use
    setGlobalGAVariables(googleAnalyticsData);

    document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent(evtName, {
            detail,
        }]
    ];
};

export const fireApplicationFinishEvent = (evtName: AnalyticsEvent, insuranceAppSummary?:IInsuranceAppSummary)=>{
    let detail: IAnalyticsApplication;
    if(!insuranceAppSummary){
        const stepName = getAppFinishEventStepName();
        detail = {
            segmentTitle: stepName,
        } as IAnalyticsApplication;
    else {
        const { googleAnalyticsData, resultCode, recordstate } = insuranceAppSummary;
        const stepName = getAppFinishEventStepName(resultCode, recordstate);
        const GAPolicyStatus = getAppFinishEventPolicyStatus(resultCode, recordstate);
        detail = {
            productCode: googleAnalyticsData.productCode ll -',
            channel: googleAnalyticsData.channel  'Web',
            policystatus: GAPolicystatus ll -',
            premiumAmount: googleAnalyticsData.premiumAmount Il -',
            coverageAmount: googleAnalyticsData.coverageAmount Il -',
            segmentTitle: stepName,
        };

        // set global variable for GTM to use
        setGlobalGAVariables(googleAnalyticsData, stepName);
    }

    document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent(evtName, {
            detail,
        }]
    ];
};

export const fireAdTraxEvent = (evtName: AnalyticsEvent, adTraxNumber: string = -') => {
    const detail: IAnalyticsAdTrax = {
        adTraxValue: adTraxNumber,
    };

    document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent(evtName, {
            detail,
        }]
    ];
};

export const fireUserDataEvent = (evtName: AnalyticsEvent, key: string, value: string) => {
    const detail: IAnalyticsUserData = {
        [key]: value,
    };

    document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent(evtName, {
            detail,
        }]
    ];
};

export const fireApplicationInputChangeEvent = (
    evtName: AnalyticsEvent,
    fieldName: string,
    fieldAction: string.
    insuranceApp?: IInsuranceApp,
    delay?: boolean,
    allowDuplicateEvents: boolean = true
] => {
    if (!insuranceApp ll !fieldName ll !fieldAction) return;

    const { segment, googleAnalyticsData } = insuranceApp;
    const { segmentTitle } = segment;

    const detail: IAnalyticsAppInputChange = {
        productCode: googleAnalyticsData.productCode ll ',
        channel: googleAnalyticsData.channel ll 'Web',
        premiumAmount: googleAnalyticsData.premiumAmount ll ',
        coverageAmount: googleAnalyticsData.coverageAmount Il -',
        segmentTitle: segmentTitle ll -',
        fieldName,
        fieldAction,
    };

    /l skip sending event if it is a duplicate
    if (!allowDuplicateEvents && analyticsInputChangeUtils.eventIsDuplicate(evtName, detail)) {
        return;
    }

      delay dispatch of the event to ensure dataLayer is fully set
    // this basically ensures clicking on add/remove repeatable fieldGroup buttons
    // does not cause duplicate events to fire due to trackotherButtons firing prior
    // to handleInputChange/handleBlur events
    if(delay){
        setTimeout(() => {
            document.dispatchEvent(
                new CustomEvent(evtName, {
                    detail,
                }}
            };
        {, 2000);
        return;
    }

    document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent(evtName, {
            detail,
        }]
    ];
};

export const fireAppstepperButtonclickEvent = (evtName: AnalyticsEvent, detail: IAnalyticsAppInputChange)=>{
    // update global variable for GTM to use
    if (window.trustData){
        window.trustData.coverageAmount = detail.coverageAmount;
        window.trustData.premiumAmount = detail.premiumAmount;
    }

    document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent(evtName, {

};

    insuranceApp?: IInsuranceApp
] => {
    if (!insuranceApp ll !fieldName) return;

    const { segment, googleAnalyticsData } = insuranceApp;
    const { segmentTitle } = segment;

    const fieldData = segment.fieldGroups
        .flatMap((group) => group.dataFields)
        .find((field) => field?.fieldCode === fieldName);

    const detail: IAnalyticsFieldError = {
        productCode: googleAnalyticsData.productCode ll ',

        coverageAmount: googleAnalyticsData.coverageAmount Il -',
        segmentTitle: segmentTitle ll ',
        fieldName: (fieldData as IAnalyticsDataField)?.googleAnalytics?.fieldName ll fieldName,
        errorValue,
    };

    // global "trustData" variable is already set from fireApplicationEvent running
      so we don't need to set it for this event

    document.dispatchEvent(
        new CustomEvent(evtName, {
            detail,
        }]
    ];
};

};

const isInsuranceApp = (obj: any): obj is IInsuranceApp => 'segment' in obj &&'googleAnalyticsData' in obj;

### 268.‘C:\DevCode\DIxUI\src\services\Analytics\inputChangeUtils.ts'

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\DIXUI\src\services\Analytics\inputChangeUtils.ts" type=".ts" size="13447" lines="330" chars="13447" -->
  typescript
/* eslint-disable class-methods-use-this */
import { AnalyticsDataFieldoptionsType, IInsuranceApp } from 'types/customTypes';
import { isEqual  from 'lodash';
import { isJsonstring } from 'utils/isJsonstring';
import { LoanApplicationEntities } from 'commonui';
import { fireApplicationInputChangeEvent } from './fireEventutils';
import { IAnalyticsAppInputChange, IAnalyticsDataField } from './types';
import { AnalyticsEvent } from './index';

class AnalyticsInputChangeUtils {
    public runAnalytics = (
        fieldCode: string.
        value: string,
        currentFormData: Record<string, string>,
        analyticsDataFieldMap: Map<string, IAnalyticsDataField>,
        insuranceApp: IInsuranceApp I undefined,
        initialLoad: boolean,
        isOnBlurChange: boolean,
        onBlurArgs?: any
    ] => {
        const previousFormData = currentFormData;
        const formData = { ...currentFormData, [fieldCode]: value };

const formFieldCode = this.getFormFieldCodeForInputChangeEvent(fieldCode, formData, analyticsDataFieldMap):
        const analyticsDataField = analyticsDataFieldMap.get(formFieldCode);
        if (analyticsDataField){
            const isOnBlurOptionsType = this.isOnBlurOptionsType(analyticsDataField);
if ((isOnBlurChange && !isOnBlurOptionsType)l(!isOnBlurChange && isOnBlurOptionsType)){
                return;
            }
            const eventData = this.getAnalyticsInputChangeEventData(
                analyticsDataField,
                previousFormData[formFieldCode],
                formData[formFieldCode],
                initialLoad,
                onBlurArgs
            };
            if(eventData){
                fireApplicationInputChangeEvent(
                    eventData.eventName,
                    eventData.fieldName,
                    eventData.fieldAction,
                    insuranceApp,
                    false,
                    eventData.allowDuplicateEvents
                };
            }
        }
    };

    public runInitialloadAnalytics = (
        formData: Record<string, string>,
        analyticsDataFieldMap: Map<string, IAnalyticsDataField>,
        insuranceApp: IInsuranceApp I undefined
    ] => {
        Object.entries(formData)
            .filter(([, valuel) => value)
            .forEach(([fieldCode]) => {
                this.runAnalytics(
                    fieldCode,
                    formData[fieldCode],
                    formData,
                    analyticsDataFieldMap,
                    insuranceApp,
                    true,
                    true
                };
            }];
    };

    public getFormFieldCodeForInputChangeEvent = (
        fieldCode: string.
        formData: Record<string, string>,
        analyticsDataFieldMap: Map<string, IAnalyticsDataField>
    ): string => {
        let formFieldCode = fieldCode;
        const analyticsDataField = analyticsDataFieldMap.get(formFieldCode);
        analyticsDataFieldMap.forEach((val, key) => {
            const swapFieldCode = val?.googleAnalytics.swapField?.fieldCode;
            const swapFieldValue = val?.googleAnalytics.swapField?.value;
const swapDisqualifier = analyticsDataField?.googleAnalytics.swapField?.swapDisqualifierValue;
const shouldSwap = swapDisqualifier && swapDisqualifier === formData[formFieldCode] && formData[key];
            if (swapFieldCode === formFieldCode && swapFieldValue === formData[formFieldCode]) {
                if (!swapDisqualifier ll shouldSwap) {
                    // swap fieldCodes in order to handle MODIFY_VALUE rules which update
                    // multiple fields at a time (lastUpdatedField may not match actual
                    // fieldCode user changed)
                    formFieldCode = key;
                }
            }
        }];
        return formFieldCode;
    };

    public getApplicationInputChangeDataLayerData = (
        eventName: AnalyticsEvent I string,
        detail: IAnalyticsAppInputChange
    ] => {
        return
            event: eventName,
stepName: detail.segmentTitle.replace('&', 'and'), // sending symbols in the data might mess up things in GTM when using Regex comparisions
            channel: detail.channel,
            productCode: detail.productCode,
            premiumAmount: detail.premiumAmount,
            coverageAmount: detail.coverageAmount,
            fieldName: detail.fieldName,
            fieldAction: detail.fieldAction,
              ..(detail.riderProductCode && { 'rider.productCode': detail.riderProductCode ),
...(detail.riderCoverageAmount && { 'rider.coverageAmount': detail.riderCoverageAmount }),
...(detail.riderPremiumAmount && { 'rider.premiumAmount': detail.riderPremiumAmount
        };
    };

    public getAnalyticsInputChangeEventData = (

return analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics.optionsType === AnalyticsDataFieldOptionsType.INPUT_STATUS:
    };

    private getFieldParameters = (
        analyticsDataField: IAnalyticsDataField,
        currentValue: string,
        newValue: string,
        initialLoad: boolean,

    ]; {
        fieldName: string;

    } => {

return this.containsOptionsType(analyticsDataField, currentValue, newValue, initialLoad);
            case AnalyticsDataFieldOptionsType.INPUT_STATUS:
                return this.useInputstatusoptionsType(
                    analyticsDataField,
                    currentValue,
                    newValue,
                    initialLoad,
                    onBlurArgs
                };
            case AnalyticsDataFieldOptionsType.LABEL:
                return this.labeloptionsType(analyticsDataField, newValue, initialLoad);
            default:
                return this.defaultoptionsType(analyticsDataField, newValue, initialLoad);
        }
    };

    private labeloptionsType = (
        analyticsDataField: IAnalyticsDataField,
        newValue: string,
        initialLoad: boolean
    ): { fieldName: string; fieldAction: string  => {
        const { fieldName } = analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics;
        let fieldAction = -';
        if (analyticsDataField.options?.length) {
            for (let i = O; i < analyticsDataField.options.length; i++) {
                const option = analyticsDataField.options[i];
                if (newValue === option.value) {
                    fieldAction = option.label ll -';
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        if(initialLoad){
            fieldAction = -';
        }
        return { fieldName, fieldAction };
    };

    private useInputstatusoptionsType = (
        analyticsDataField: IAnalyticsDataField,
        currentValue: string,
        newValue: string,
        initialLoad: boolean,
        onBlurArgs?: any
    ): { fieldName: string; fieldAction: string; allowDuplicateEvents: boolean  => {
        let { fieldName } = analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics;
        let previousValue = currentValue;
        let nextValue = newValue;
        let fieldAction = -';
        let allowDuplicateEvents = true;
        if (fieldName === 'Height'){
            if(onBlurArgs){
                if (onBlurArgs.isFeet) {
                    fieldName = 'HeightFeet';
previousValue = isJsonstring(previousValue) ? JsoN.parse(previousValue).feet : -';
                    nextValue = isJsonstring(nextValue) ? JsoN.parse(nextValue).feet : -'
                else {
                    fieldName = 'HeightInches';
previousValue = isJsonstring(previousValue) ? JsoN.parse(previousValue).inches : -';
                    nextValue = isJsonstring(nextValue) ? JsoN.parse(nextValue).inches : '
                }
            }
        }
        if(initialload){
            fieldAction = 'Prefilled';
        else if (!!previousValue && previousValue !== nextValue) {
            fieldAction = 'Updated'
            allowDuplicateEvents = false;
        } else if (!!previousValue && previousValue === nextValue) {
            fieldAction = ;
        } else if (!previousValue && nextValue) {
            fieldAction = 'Completed';
        } else if (!nextValue) {
            fieldAction = 'Skipped';
        }

        return { fieldName, fieldAction, allowDuplicateEvents ;
    };

    private containsoptionsType = (
        analyticsDataField: IAnalyticsDataField,
        currentValue: string,
        newValue: string,
        initialLoad: boolean
    ): { fieldName: string; fieldAction: string } => {
        let modifiedFieldName = analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics.fieldName;
        let fieldAction = ';
        for (let i = O; i < analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics.options.length; i++) {
            const option = analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics.options[il;
            const currentValueIncludesoption = currentValue.includes(option.value);
            const newValueIncludesOption = newValue.includes(option.value);
            if (currentValueIncludesOption && !newValueIncludesOption && option.actionNegate) {
                if (option.fieldName){
                    modifiedFieldName = option.fieldName;
                }
                fieldAction = option.actionNegate;
            }
            if (!currentValueIncludesOption && newValueIncludesOption){
                if (option.fieldName)){
                    modifiedFieldName = option.fieldName;
                }
                fieldAction = option.action;
            }
        }
        if (initialLoad){
            fieldAction = -';
        }
        return  fieldName: modifiedFieldName, fieldAction };
    };

    private defaultoptionsType = (
        analyticsDataField: IAnalyticsDataField,
        newValue: string,
        initialLoad: boolean
    ): { fieldName: string; fieldAction: string  => {
        const { fieldName } = analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics;
        let fieldAction = -';
        for (let i = O; i < analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics.options.length; i++) {
            const option = analyticsDataField.googleAnalytics.options[il;
            if (newValue === option.value) {
                fieldAction = option.action;
                break; // once we find a match we can break the loop
            }
        }
        if(initialLoad){
            fieldAction = ;
        }
        return { fieldName, fieldAction };
    };
}

```
    RIDER_SELECTED = 'riderSelected',

}

[>
* The following interfaces that start with IAnalytics are all of the data that
* we utilize intermittently before we send the data to google analytics. The
   event name is tied together in a CustomEvent when we fire the event to be
   picked up in the AnalyticsService. Most of the fields are what they are when
* we send them to GA; however, segmentTitle gets mapped to stepName and some of
* the field names may have a period separator in them.
>[

}

* correlating interface that would then get properly mapped and sent to google
* analytics. This is wrapped in the IGTMPayload interface, which is the final
```

class AnalyticsUserDatautils {
public runInputChange = async (fieldCode: string, value: string, validationErrors: Record<string, string>) => {
        if (!value ll fieldCode in validationErrors) return;
        let key:
        let unhashedKey;
        switch (fieldCode) {
            case FieldCode.ADDRESS_MAIL_CITY:
                key = 't.c';
                break;
            case FieldCode.ADDRESS_MAIL_LINE1:
                key = 't.a';
                break;
            case FieldCode.ADDRESS_MAIL_STATE:
                key = 't.s';
                break;
            case FieldCode.ADDRESS_MAIL_ZIP:
                key = 't.z';
                break;
            case FieldCode.EMAIL_ADDRESS:
                key = 't.e';
                unhashedKey = 'user.email';
                break;
            case FieldCode.FIRST_NAME:
                key = 't.f';
                break;
            case FieldCode.HOME_PHONE:
                key = 't.p';
                break;
            case FieldCode.LAST_NAME:
                key = 't.l';
                break;
            default:
        }
        if (key){
            const normalizedValue = this.normalizeValue(key, value);
            const hashedValue = await this.hashValue(normalizedValue);
            fireUserDataEvent(AnalyticsEvent.USER_DATA, key, hashedValue):
        }
        if (unhashedKey)
            fireUserDataEvent(AnalyticsEvent.USER_DATA, unhashedKey, value);
        }
    };

    public runInitialLoad = async (googleAnalyticsData: IGoogleAnalyticsData) => {
        let dobYear;
        let dobMonth;
        let dobDay;
        if (googleAnalyticsData.dateofBirth)
            const [year, month, day] = googleAnalyticsData.dateofBirth.split('-');
            dobYear = year;
            dobMonth = month;
            dobDay = day;
        }
        const userData: { [k: string]: string } = {
              ..(googleAnalyticsData.email && { 't.e': googleAnalyticsData.email }),
              ..(googleAnalyticsData.gender && { 't.g': googleAnalyticsData.gender ),
               .(googleAnalyticsData.phone && { 't.p': googleAnalyticsData.phone }),
              .(googleAnalyticsData.firstName && { 't.f': googleAnalyticsData.firstName ),
              ..(googleAnalyticsData.lastName && { 't.l': googleAnalyticsData.lastName ),
...(googleAnalyticsData.addressLine1 && { 't.a': googleAnalyticsData.addressLine1
               .(googleAnalyticsData.city && { 't.c': googleAnalyticsData.city }),
              .(googleAnalyticsData.state && { 't.s': googleAnalyticsData.state }),
              .(googleAnalyticsData.zip && { 't.z': googleAnalyticsData.zip }),
              ..(dobYear && { 't.y': dobYear }),
              ..(dobMonth && { 't.m': dobMonth }),
              ..(dobDay && { 't.d': dobDay }),
        };
        const normalizedUserData = this.normalizeValues(userData);
        const hashedUserData = await this.hashValues(normalizedUserData);

        Object.keys(hashedUserData).forEach((key) => {
            fireUserDataEvent(AnalyticsEvent.USER_DATA, key, hashedUserData[key]);
        }];
    };

private hashvalues = async (data: { [k: string]: string }): Promise<l [k: string]: string
        const userData = { ...data };
        await Promise.all(
            Object.keys(userData).map(async (key) => {
                userData[key] = await this.hashValue(userData[key] as string);
            }]
        };
        return userData;
    };

    private normalizeValues = (data: { [k: string]: string ): { [k: string]: string } => {
        const userData = { ...data };
        Object.keys(userData).forEach((key) => {
            userData[key] = this.normalizeValue(key, userData[key]);
        }];
        return userData;
    };

    public normalizeValue = (key: string, value: string): string => {
        let modifiedValue = value.toLowerCase().trim();
        switch (key){
            case 't.g':
modifiedValue = modifiedValue === 'male' ll modifiedValue.startswith('m') ? 'm' :-f
                break;
            case 't.p':
                modifiedValue = formatPhoneNumber(modifiedValue, true, false, true);
                break;
            case 't.c':
                modifiedValue = modifiedValue.replace(/[^a-z]/g, -');
                break;
            case 't.f':
            case 't.l': {
                const punctuation = /[\p{P}]/u;
                modifiedValue = modifiedValue.replace(punctuation, -');
                break;
            }
            case 't.z':
                modifiedValue = modifiedValue.slice(0, 5);
                break;
            default:
                break;
        }
        return modifiedValue.trim():
    };

    private hashValue = async (value: string): Promise<string> => {
        const msgUint8 = new TextEncoder().encode(value); // encode as (utf-8) Uint8Array
        const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgUint8); // hash the value
const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer)); // convert buffer to byte array
const hashHex = hashArray.map((b) => b.tostring(16).padstart(2, 'o')).join(-'); //convert bytes to hex string
        return hashHex;
    };
}

```
import { ResultCodes, Recordstate } from 'types/customTypes';
import { getCookieValue, formatGACookieValue  from 'utils/cookieUtils';

export const setGlobalGAVariables = (googleAnalyticsData: Record<string, any>, summaryStatus?:string) => {
    window.truStData = {
        appSubstatus: googleAnalyticsData.appSubstatus,
        contNumber: googleAnalyticsData.contNumber,
        coverageAmount: googleAnalyticsData.coverageAmount,
        correlationId: googleAnalyticsData.correlationId,
        gender: googleAnalyticsData.gender,
        gaCookieID: formatGACookieValue(getCookieValue('_ga')),
        id: googleAnalyticsData.id,
        insuredstatus: googleAnalyticsData.insuredstatus,
        issueAge: googleAnalyticsData.issueAge,
        offerNumber: googleAnalyticsData.offerNumber,
        offerQuoteId: googleAnalyticsData.offerQuoteId,
        orgName: googleAnalyticsData.orgName,
        orgNumber: googleAnalyticsData.orgNumber,
        paymentFreg: googleAnalyticsData.paymentFreq.
        paymentMethod: googleAnalyticsData.paymentMethod,
        policyNumber: googleAnalyticsData.policyNumber,
        policyStatus: googleAnalyticsData.policystatus,
        premiumAmount: googleAnalyticsData.premiumAmount,
        productCode: googleAnalyticsData.productCode,
        state: googleAnalyticsData.state,
        zipCode: googleAnalyticsData.zip,
        dob: googleAnalyticsData.dateofBirth,
        city: googleAnalyticsData.city.
        partnerType: googleAnalyticsData.partnerType,
        partnerCode: googleAnalyticsData.partnerCode,
        summarystatus,
    };
};

export const getAppFinishEventstepName = (resultCode?: number, recordstate?: string) => {
    let stepName = 'End Session Generic';

    switch (resultCode){
        case ResultCodes.APPROVED: {
            if (recordState !== RecordState.IN_PROGRESS)
                stepName = 'Success';
            }
            break;
        }
        case ResultCodes.PENDING:
            stepName = 'Pending';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.DECLINED:
            stepName = 'Decline';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.DECLINED_GAWL_PIVOT:
            stepName = 'Declined GAwL Pivot';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.PRIOR_DECLINED:
            stepName = 'Prior Decline';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.BAD_ACTOR:
        case ResultCodes.BAD_ACTOR_SECONDARY:
            stepName = 'Bad Actor';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.ID_VERIFICATION:
            stepName = 'ID Verification Failed';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.NOT_THE_INSURED:
            stepName = 'close';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.NO_REPLACEMENTS:
            stepName = 'No Replacements';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.STOLI_KNOCKOUT:
            stepName = 'STOLI Knockout';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.ADBO_KNOCKOUT:
            stepName = 'ADBO Knockout';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.COVERAGE_MAXIMUM:
            stepName = 'Coverage Max';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.RX_FAILURE:
            stepName = 'RX Decline';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.SESSION_TIMEOUT:
            stepName = 'Session Timed Out'
            break;
        case ResultCodes.SYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE:
        case ResultCodes.PROCESSING_ERRORS:
            stepName = 'System Errors';
            break;
        default:
    }
    return stepName;
};
export const getAppFinishEventPolicystatus = (resultCode?: number, recordstate?: string) => {
    let PolicyStatus = 'Pending';

    switch(resultCode){
        case ResultCodes.APPROVED: {
            if (recordState !== RecordState.IN_PROGRESS) {
                Policystatus = 'Approved';
            }
            break;
        }
        case ResultCodes.PENDING:
            break;
        case ResultCodes.DECLINED:
            PolicyStatus = 'Denied';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.DECLINED_GAWL_PIVOT:
            PolicyStatus = 'Denied';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.PRIOR_DECLINED:
            PolicyStatus = 'Prior Declined';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.BAD_ACTOR:
        case ResultCodes.BAD_ACTOR_SECONDARY:
            PolicyStatus = 'Bad Actor';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.ID_VERIFICATION:
            Policystatus = 'Denied';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.NOT_THE_INSURED:
            PolicyStatus = 'Not the insured';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.NO_REPLACEMENTS:
            PolicyStatus = 'Denied';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.STOLI_KNOCKOUT:
            PolicyStatus = 'Denied';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.ADBO_KNOCKOUT:
            PolicyStatus = 'Denied';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.COVERAGE_MAXIMUM:
            PolicyStatus = 'Coverage Maximum';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.RX_FAILURE:
            PolicyStatus = 'Denied';
            break;
        case ResultCodes.SESSION_TIMEOUT:
            break;
        case ResultCodes.SYSTEM_UNAVAILABLE:
        case ResultCodes.PROCESSING_ERRORS:
            PolicyStatus = 'Service Down';
            break;
        default:
    }
    return Policystatus;
};
```

/* eslint-disable class-methods-use-this */
import { FieldGroupCode } from 'types/customTypes';
import { fireApplicationAddressScrubbingEvent  from './fireEventutils';
import { AnalyticsEvent } from './index';

class AnalyticsVerifyAddressUtils {
    public runAnalytics = (groupCode: string, value: string, isVerifiedAddress?: boolean) => {
        let eventData;
        switch(value){
            case 'foundAddress':
            case 'noAddress':
                eventData = this.getAddressModalDisplayEventData(groupCode, value)
                break;
            case 'addressSelection':
eventData = this.getAddressModalSelectionEventData(groupCode, isVerifiedAddress);
                break;
            case 'closedAddressModal':
                eventData = this.getAddressModalDisplayEventData(groupCode, value);
                break;
            default:
                break;
        }
         if (eventData){
fireApplicationAddressScrubbingEvent(eventData.eventName, eventData.action, eventData.label);
        }
    };

    private getAddressModalSelectionEventData = (
        groupCode: string.
        isVerifiedAddress?: boolean
    ): { eventName: AnalyticsEvent; action: string; label: string  | undefined => {
        const eventName = AnalyticsEvent.ADDRESS_SCRUBBING;
        const action = isVerifiedAddress ? 'Selected Suggested Address' : 'Selected Entered
        let label = ';
        if ((groupCode === FieldGroupCode.CONTACT_INFO) {
            label = 'Mailing Address';
        }
        if (groupCode === FieldGroupCode.SECONDARY_ADDRESSEE) {
            label = 'Secondary Addressee';
        }
        if (eventName && action && label) {
            return { eventName, action, label };
        }
        return undefined;
    };

    private getAddressModalDisplayEventData = (
        groupCode: string.
        value?: string
    ): { eventName: AnalyticsEvent; action: string; label: string  | undefined => {
        const eventName = AnalyticsEvent.ADDRESS_SCRUBBING;
        let action = -';
        let label = ';
        if ((groupCode === FieldGroupCode.CONTACT_INFO) {
            label = 'Mailing Address';
        }
        if (groupCode === FieldGroupCode.SECONDARY_ADDRESSEE) {
            label = 'Secondary Addressee';
        }
        if (value === 'foundAddress'){
            action = 'Displayed';
        }
        if (value === 'noAddress'){
            action = 'Address Not Found';
        }
          f(value ==='closedAddressModal'){
            action = 'Back Button Click';
        }
        if (eventName && action && label) {
            return { eventName, action, label ;
        }
        return undefined;
    };
}

export const analyticsVerifyAddressUtils = new AnalyticsVerifyAddressutils();

